# Skills · G · part 36

868 entries, genre-essentials to geo-database. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **genre-essentials**: Explains what makes a genre sound like itself — the harmonic, melodic, rhythmic, and timbral signatures of blues, jazz, pop, modal, folk, and others. Pure catalog skill. Use when a learner asks 'what makes blues blues' / 'what defines jazz harmony' / 'how do I write a country progression'. ([GuitarAlchemist/ga](https://github.com/GuitarAlchemist/ga/tree/HEAD/skills/genre-essentials) · ★2)
- **genre-expert**: Answers any question about a music genre or subgenre: what it is, how two genres differ (e.g. drill vs trap, house vs techno), where it came from, its defining sound, tempo and instruments, the key producers, artists and records, its subgenres, and how to make or recognise it. Use for every genre question, comparison or listening request. ([Vocaler-AI/music-skills](https://github.com/Vocaler-AI/music-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/genre-expert) · ★3)
- **genre-fantasy**: Fantasy genre conventions: magic systems, worldbuilding principles, fantasy tropes and narrative structures, subgenre distinctions. Use when planning or writing fantasy fiction. ([Thomashighbaugh/buchbinder](https://github.com/Thomashighbaugh/buchbinder/tree/HEAD/src/skills/fiction/genre-knowledge/fantasy))
